- **log-activity**: The lifecycle's single logger. After every lifecycle step, append one machine-readable record to docs/activity_log.jsonl. A server channel is not a group chat. DMs and group DMs are [channelReply](channelReply.md). ([matthewdonsemail-lab/log](https://github.com/matthewdonsemail-lab/log/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/log-discord-server-reply) · ★68 · 3 copies)
